# In pursuance of Regulation 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1988, it is hereby notified that in exercise of the powers

NOTIFICATION New Delhi, the 26th August, 2023 (Chartered Accountants) 3SCA(4)/1/2021-2022.—In pursuance of Regulation 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1988, it is hereby notified that in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 20(1) (c) of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, the Council of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India has removed from the Register of Members of this Institute with effect from 1st January 2022 on account of non-payment of prescribed fees, the names of the following persons:
